shard: `catalogue-portals`

## Method and source pins

The APIs.guru result is the already-committed sweep at snapshot `2026-09-08T20:48:30Z`, catalogue digest `dfac835d2d1f13dfdb82723d72be1acf567df53c1b710bf6d68e28b795696e66`; it was not re-fetched for another census. Existing corpus rows establish that `https://api.apis.guru/v2/specs/<api>/<version>/openapi.json` plus the catalogue version is an admissible immutable reference form. The downloaded bytes for the two named candidates were additionally hashed: GitHub Enterprise `81270616a62bcd4b06e776f7dea06493995c2450332cd2b5a3708c5ebb01a06e`, Zoom `cdfd27047bb0e4f7038135605d9aa4e72eeb17d86f2195b5f92f09f76191ce18`.

The exact inputs are committed in [`catalogue-portals-sources.tsv`](catalogue-portals-sources.tsv), and the exact positive output is [`catalogue-portals-declarers.tsv`](catalogue-portals-declarers.tsv). Download the jentic archive named by the manifest into `$JENTIC_ROOT`, download each answered vendor row below `$VENDOR_ROOT` preserving its `name/path`, then run the commands recorded in the table. The committed runner imports `compile_conjunction` and `census_document` from `scripts/openapi-surface-census.py`; it does not reimplement selector semantics.

The jentic query read all **74,232 JSON objects** at commit `eb9d12a2684b0fbcb5aecf51e8ae54dba0929743` with zero unreadable. Appendix B deliberately includes the four import representations and primary `openapi.json` separately when each declares the shape. Each disposition is specific to its API/version source artifact: it records a content digest and links the pinned `apis.json` plus `meta/meta.json` import trace used to establish a publisher source, a third-party source, a transformation, or the absence of an origin. The immutable aggregation commit and its redistribution grant, independently recorded in the [existing corpus provenance ledger](../../../tests/fixtures/CORPUS.md), do not by themselves repair missing publisher provenance. Import representations inherit a primary Fern result only when their parsed JSON equals both the primary and the preserved `meta/source/source.dat`; a different representation is screened separately if it equals the publisher source, and otherwise fails publisher ownership. For successful generations, retention was inspected in the generated model and client bodies at the exact declaration sites: union aliases and member annotations prove the `anyOf`/`oneOf` behavior, Pydantic `extra` proves whether a closed-object constraint survived, and `typing.Any` at the corresponding endpoint/model field proves Fern's handling of an undeclared component head. The rerun produced 681 jentic declarer rows and 10 vendor declarer rows, exactly equal as sets to the committed declarer ledger.

The vendor query used the 25 publisher paths pinned by the prior search. Twenty-one parsed; Twilio's document was unanswered because the census YAML reader rejects its explicit-key mapping, Dropbox remains 404, ElevenLabs has no immutable reference in the prior record, and the Docker documentation path is not an OpenAPI document. Therefore a key with no declaration among the 21 parsed documents is `unanswered`, never zero. The exact publisher commits for every positive hit are in Appendix C.
